We evaluate both statement and proof translation across five frontier and open-weight LLMs on 12 directed translation pairs: statement translation peaks at 29.1% pass@1 and proof translation at 10.5%; controlled theorems reach 29.7% proof pass@1 versus 5.2% for ecosystem-level translations, confirming that library mismatch is the dominant bottleneck. In addition to pass@k evaluation, a deterministic Lean 4 BEq check establishes equivalence for 54.0% of verified source-to-Lean 4 miniF2F statement translations, showing that native type-checking alone can substantially overestimate semantic fidelity; in an autoformalization/auto-informalization round-trip study, Rocq and HOL Light are easier formalization targets than Lean 4 and Isabelle, while multi-ITP context improves pooled Lean 4 success from 4.8% to 10.6%. Our benchmark, verification infrastructure, and evaluation pipelines are publicly released.

</details>


### [142] [FORCE-Bench: A Benchmark, Dataset, and Evaluation Harness for Agentic AI in Enterprise Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19409)
*Wolfgang M. Pauli,Sarah Panda,Kidus Admassu,Said Bleik,Ademola Okerinde,Jeremy Reynolds*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FORCE-Bench，一个针对运营金融工作流的基准测试，包含251个专家标注查询，评估智能体在准确性、可验证性等方面的表现。结果显示通用智能体难以满足金融领域质量要求，而专用Finance Agent更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要关注通用能力、指令遵循或安全性，缺乏对运营金融工作流中智能体实际部署需求的直接评估。金融专业人士需要智能体提供事实准确、有依据且符合领域规则和约束的信息。

Method: 构建FORCE-Bench基准，涵盖251个专家标注查询，基于八个维度（准确性、引用、清晰度、深度、 groundedness、时效性、相关性、结构）进行评分。评估三类任务：财务义务研究、财务实体绩效研究和商业简报生成。在工具访问和延迟受限条件下，评估专用智能体和通用智能体。

Result: 通用目的智能体在运营约束下未能一致地满足金融领域的质量要求；专为Microsoft 365 Copilot设计的Finance Agent在多个维度上表现更可靠。

Conclusion: FORCE-Bench为评估企业金融环境中的智能体提供了开源数据集、评分标准、工具和代码，支持可复现的比较和适应性调整，强调专用智能体在特定垂直领域的重要性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have accelerated deployment of agentic systems in operational finance. Existing benchmarks emphasize measuring general capabilities, instruction following, or safety, but few directly address the operational finance workflows that agentic systems are now being deployed to automate. Finance professionals require agents to not only provide factually sound and properly grounded information, but also ensure that this information is verifiable and consistently adheres to rules and constraints of the operational finance domain. We introduce FORCE-Bench, which contains 251 expert-annotated queries and evaluates responses using a rubric-based framework calibrated to the requirements of the operational finance domain, across eight dimensions: accuracy, citations, clarity, depth, groundedness, recency, relevance, and structure. FORCE-Bench assesses agentic systems on three task types: financial obligation research (querying ERP systems for accounts receivable and payable data), financial entity performance research (answering time-bound questions from public filings and market data), and business brief generation (synthesising multi-source company intelligence reports). To reflect real deployment conditions, we evaluate our purpose-built agent, as well as the general-purpose agentic systems, under common tool access and latency-bounded settings. Results show that general-purpose agentic systems do not consistently meet finance-domain quality requirements under operational constraints, while the purpose-built Finance Agent for Microsoft 365 Copilot is more reliable across dimensions. We release the dataset, rubrics, harness, and analysis code as open-source to support reproducible comparison and adaptation to other enterprise finance environments.

</details>


### [143] [The Chronos Vulnerability: A Taxonomy of Temporal Persistence and Memory-Based Deception in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19433)
*Om Narayan,Ramkinker Singh,Praveen Baskar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究揭示了从无状态生成模型向有状态自主代理转变带来的“Chronos漏洞”，即基于记忆的持久化攻击（如MINJA和休眠代理）。传统端点过滤器对此无效，论文提出了包含AgentDoG、Agent-C、A-MemGuard及硬件TEE等多层防御体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI从生成模型演变为具备长期规划和自动化工作流能力的有状态自主代理，出现了一种新型安全威胁——Chronos漏洞。该漏洞通过记忆注入等手段破坏代理的内部信念系统，使攻击向量与最终灾难性事件解耦，而现有安全措施无法有效应对这一架构变化带来的风险。

Method: 在“工作流世界”（World of Workflows）基准测试背景下，形式化了基于持久性的攻击威胁模型及动态盲区（Dynamics Blindness）威胁；评估了传统端点内容过滤器的局限性；综合并分类了新兴的纵深防御框架，包括诊断轨迹护栏、形式化时序验证、免疫记忆共识及硬件锚定信任机制。

Result: 证明了传统端点内容过滤器不足以保护当前的有状态架构；识别出Memory Injection Attack (MINJA) 和休眠代理作为主要攻击形式；构建了涵盖软件算法（AgentDoG, Agent-C, A-MemGuard）和硬件安全（GPU TEEs, Zero-Trust memory）的综合防御景观。

Conclusion: 面对有状态自主代理引入的新型内存基础攻击，必须超越传统的静态内容过滤，采用结合诊断、验证、共识及硬件可信执行环境的纵深防御策略，以保障企业工作流自动化的安全性。

Abstract: The transition from stateless generative models in artificial intelligence to stateful, autonomous agents represents an architectural evolution that, while providing the capabilities of long-term planning and the automation of enterprise workflows, also represents the introduction of a new form of security threat, the Chronos Vulnerability. The Chronos Vulnerability represents the threat of memory-based attacks, including the Memory Injection Attack (MINJA) and the sleeper agent, in which the internal belief system of the autonomous agent is compromised, effectively decoupling the attack vector from the final catastrophic event. This study formalizes the threat model for persistence-based attacks and the threat of Dynamics Blindness in the context of the World of Workflows benchmark, demonstrating that traditional endpoint content filters are insufficient for the current stateful architecture. Consequently, this study synthesizes a defense-in-depth landscape, categorizing emerging frameworks such as diagnostic trajectory guardrails (AgentDoG), formal temporal verification (Agent-C), immunological memory consensus (A-MemGuard), and hardware-anchored trust via GPU-based Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) and Zero-Trust memory architectures.

</details>


### [144] [Sophisticated Policies from Epistemic Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19518)
*Wouter W. L. Nuijten,Bert de Vries*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了Sophisticated Inference的核心优势并非源于其复杂的树搜索结构，而是源于其闭环推断特性。通过在Epistemic-prior变分自由能框架下分解认知先验与联合后验，研究证明只有结合“认知驱动力”与“未来动作依赖未来状态”的闭环结构，才能有效解决复杂环境中的信息获取与目标达成问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Sophisticated Inference（一种常与递归信念建模和树搜索关联的主动推断变体）的核心计算角色，验证其优势是否特定于树搜索算法本身，还是源于更基础的闭环推断结构。

Method: 在Reactivity Maze随机基准测试中，评估三种具有相同状态-动作后验族的变分目标、动作-状态因子化的主动推断目标、Sophisticated Inference以及标准预期自由能规划方法。通过分离认知激励与内层闭环控制来比较不同方法的性能。

Result: 单一成分不足：缺乏认知组件的方法无法寻求信息；阻止未来动作依赖未来状态的方法无法将信息转化为可靠的目标达成。相比之下，Sophisticated Inference和完整的联合epistemic-prior主动推断均通过结合认知驱动与闭环推断成功解决了环境任务。

Conclusion: Sophisticated Inference的优势并不依赖于树搜索本身，而是源于主动推断的闭环形式。这种形式可以在epistemic-prior变分推断中表示，只要后验分布保持未来动作对未来状态的依赖性即可实现高效推理与控制。

Abstract: Sophisticated Inference is a variant of active inference often associated with recursive belief modeling and tree search. We argue that its central computational role is simpler: within a planning horizon, it makes active inference closed-loop by allowing future actions to depend on future states and observations. This closed-loop structure can be represented in the epistemic-prior variational free energy framework. Epistemic priors supply the active-inference objective, while a joint posterior over future states and actions supplies the state-contingent control structure. We evaluate this decomposition in the Reactivity Maze, a stochastic benchmark designed to separate epistemic incentive from inner-horizon closed-loop control. The comparison includes three variational objectives with the same state-action posterior family, an action-state factorized active inference objective, Sophisticated Inference, and standard Expected Free Energy planning. The results show that neither ingredient is sufficient on its own. Methods without an epistemic component do not seek information, while methods that prevent future actions from depending on future states cannot turn information into reliable goal-reaching. By contrast, both Sophisticated Inference and full-joint epistemic-prior active inference solve the environment by combining epistemic drive with closed-loop inference. These results show that the advantage associated with Sophisticated Inference need not be specific to tree search itself. It arises from the closed-loop form of active inference, and this form can be represented in epistemic-prior variational inference when the posterior keeps future actions dependent on future states.

</details>


### [145] [Knowledge-Centric Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19592)
*Xuefei Julie Wang,Lauren Hyoseo Yoon,Chengrui Qu,Amanda Zichang Wang,Atharva Sehgal,Eric Mazumdar,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“以知识为中心”的自我改进范式，通过构建可持久化、可迁移的知识库替代传统的优化智能体本身，在多个基准测试中实现了更高的解决率和更低的成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的以智能体为中心的自我改进方法（如优化提示词、工作流或代码）导致改进成果与特定智能体设计、任务分布或适应过程紧密绑定，难以维护和迁移，且维护成本高。

Method: 研究一种互补的范式：以知识为中心的自我改进。在该范式中，智能体保持通用和一次性使用，而核心改进对象是一个经过策划的持久化知识库。具体协议包括：智能体尝试任务，通过任务级和跨任务论坛贡献基于证据的见解，随后进行知识蒸馏。

Result: 在抽象推理、编码和终端基准测试中，该协议提高了任务解决率，并降低了相对于智能体中心基线的美元成本。蒸馏后的知识能够迁移到未见过的任务以及不同的LLM家族中，证明改进并非特定于某种LLM或运行环境的行为。

Conclusion: 自我改进型代理系统的进步可以主要由策划的持久性知识驱动，这种知识比智能体本身更具可检查性、可迁移性和便携性。

Abstract: Self-improving AI systems typically treat the agent as the object that improves, by optimizing prompts, workflows, harnesses, or even the agent's own code. This agent-centric view can make improvements expensive to maintain and difficult to transfer, because gains become tied to a particular agent design, task distribution, or adaptation run. We study a complementary paradigm: knowledge-centric self-improvement, in which agents remain generic and disposable while the persistent object is a curated knowledge base that agents can leverage for future tasks. We conduct controlled case studies to operationalize this idea via a simple protocol. Agents attempt one task, then contribute evidence-grounded insights to a shared knowledge base via task-level and cross-task forums, followed by knowledge distillation. Because self-improvement is contained in the knowledge rather than the agent, improvement can be more inspectable, transferable, and portable. Across abstract reasoning, coding, and terminal benchmarks, this protocol improves solve rates while reducing dollar cost relative to agent-centric baselines. The resulting distilled knowledge also transfers to held-out tasks and across LLM families, indicating that the improvement is not merely an LLM- or run-specific behavior. These results support a new view of self-improving agentic systems: progress can be driven primarily by the curated persistent knowledge. Code is available at https://github.com/recursive-knowledge/KSI.

</details>


### [146] [Edge Intelligence in Civil Aviation: Paradigms, Techniques, and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19676)
*Wenbin Li,Zhongtian Liao,Bolin Liu,Yongjie Zhou,Jingling Wu,Xiaoyong Lin,Jing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了面向民航领域的边缘智能（Edge AI），旨在解决云端AI部署带来的高延迟、离线能力缺失及数据隐私风险，通过边缘计算实现低延迟、隐私保护和弹性运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民航运营产生海量异构数据，传统云端AI部署存在任务延迟高、通信受限环境下缺乏离线能力、以及敏感数据集中带来的隐私和主权风险。

Method: 文章提供了民航边缘智能的全景视图，首先阐述操作动机，回顾边缘推理和学习技术，介绍民航环境中的组织计算范式及其配置，最后描述新兴应用和未来研究趋势。

Result: 提出了边缘解决方案可以补充云端基础架构，从而在民航全生命周期中提供低延迟、保护隐私且具有弹性的AI服务。

Conclusion: 边缘智能通过压缩、协同推理和分割学习等技术，将感知、预测和决策逻辑靠近数据源头，有效降低延迟、带宽占用和数据暴露风险，是民航领域AI发展的关键方向。

Abstract: Civil aviation is safety critical and its operations, from flight decks and towers to ramps and maintenance, generate massive, heterogeneous data at the network edge. Yet cloud centric deployment of large Artificial Intelligence (AI) models often produces high task latency, lacks offline capability in communication denied environments, and requires centralizing sensitive data, raising privacy and sovereignty risks. Edge AI moves perception, prediction, and decision logic closer to the data producers via compression, collaborative inference, and split learning, thereby reducing latency, bandwidth, and exposure while enabling graceful operation during disconnections. This paper provides a panoramic view and a common understanding of edge intelligence tailored to civil aviation. We firstly articulate the operational motivations for edge AI, and then review recent techniques for edge inference and edge learning. We then introduce the organizational computing paradigms and the respective configurations in civil aviation environments; finally, we describe the emerging applications and the future research trends of edge intelligence in civil aviation. We argue that a refined edge solution can complement cloud foundations to deliver low latency, privacy preserving, and resilient AI services across the civil aviation lifecycle.

</details>


### [147] [Symbol and Footprint Database for Electronic Components by Agentic Recognition and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19767)
*Yichen Shi,Yuzhi Liu,Zhuofu Tao,Li Huang,Yuhao Gao,Ting-Jung Lin,Lei Hel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SFgen，利用多模态大语言模型自动识别和生成PCB元件符号与焊盘，显著提升设计效率并构建SFnet数据库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCB设计中，工程师手动创建元件符号和焊盘耗时且易出错，亟需自动化解决方案以提升设计效率与准确性。

Method: 开发SFgen，一种基于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的智能体识别与生成流程，用于电子元件的符号和焊盘自动生成。

Result: SFgen在符号生成上达到86%准确率，在焊盘生成上达到80%准确率；基于此方法构建了包含1000个元件并持续扩展的SFnet数据库。

Conclusion: SFgen有效解决了PCB元件库构建的痛点，其生成的SFnet数据库为PCB设计的自动化生成奠定了坚实基础。

Abstract: A rich and recognizable component library is the cornerstone of printed circuit board (PCB) design and generation. Traditionally, engineers manually create symbols and footprints and design PCB schematics, which is time-consuming and error-prone. Leveraging multimodal large language models (MLLMs), we develop SFgen, an agentic recognition and generation flow of symbol and footprint for electronic components. SFgen achieves 86% accuracy for symbol generation and 80% accuracy for footprint generation. We use the SFgen method to create SFnet, a database of symbols and footprints. It now has 1000 components and is expanding constantly, which lays the foundation for automatic generation of PCB designs.

</details>


### [148] [Silent Failures in Multimodal Agentic Search:A Diagnostic Taxonomy and Cross-Judge Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19793)
*Zhengxian Wu,Junjie Gao,Kai Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“静默失败”概念，指出多模态代理搜索系统在最终答案准确率之外存在轨迹层面的隐蔽错误。通过构建六类分类法和轨迹级诊断管道，发现表面准确率严重高估了模型的真实能力，且这些失败具有能力依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态代理搜索系统评估主要关注最终答案的准确性，忽略了搜索轨迹中的失败模式（即“静默失败”），导致对模型真实可靠性的误判。

Method: 1. 构建涵盖模态捷径、幻影接地、错误证据正确回答、过度检索洗白、跨模态矛盾和来源幻觉的六类分类法；2. 基于该分类法建立统一的ReAct风格轨迹级诊断管道，同时评估答案正确性和证据接地质量；3. 在MMSearch-Plus轨迹上对四个前沿多模态模型进行实验，并结合交叉裁判验证、空白图像压力测试和工具消融研究。

Result: 实验表明，表面准确率一致性地高估了真实的轨迹级正确性。“静默失败”依赖于模型能力，且在干预下往往发生转移而非消失。

Conclusion: 仅依靠最终答案准确率不足以全面评估多模态代理搜索系统的可靠性，必须引入轨迹级的诊断方法以揭示隐藏的静默失败问题。

Abstract: Multimodal agentic search systems increasingly rely on external tools to answer knowledge-intensive visual questions. However, existing evaluations mainly focus on final-answer accuracy and may miss failures in the search trajectory. In this work, we study such hidden reliability issues as silent failures. We introduce a six-category taxonomy covering modality shortcuts, phantom grounding, wrong-evidence-right-answer cases, over-retrieval laundering, cross-modal contradiction, and provenance hallucination. Based on this taxonomy, we build a trajectory-level diagnostic pipeline that evaluates both answer correctness and evidence-grounding quality under a unified ReAct-style scaffold. Experiments on MMSearch-Plus trajectories across four frontier multimodal models show that surface accuracy consistently overestimates true trajectory-level correctness. We further use cross-judge validation, blank-image stress tests, and tool ablations to show that silent failures are capability-dependent and often shift rather than disappear. Home-page: https://github.com/DingWu1021/silent-failures-multimodal-agentic-search

</details>


### [149] [Rewarding Better Thinking for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19824)
*Xubo Liu,Wenya Guo,Ruxue Yan,Xinying Qian,Ying Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出思维清单奖励（TCR），一种面向过程的强化学习奖励机制，通过评估推理轨迹而非仅最终结果，解决现有偏好对齐中信用分配粗糙的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于强化学习的LLM偏好对齐方法主要依赖结果级代理奖励，缺乏对推理轨迹的指导，导致在多个响应获得相似最终得分时，轨迹级偏好定义不明确且信用分配粗糙。

Method: 提出思维清单奖励（TCR）：1. 将偏好对转化为样本特定的思维清单，用于评估生成的推理轨迹是否涵盖偏好隐含的考量；2.引入指数移动平均（EMA）残差公式，隔离出超出结果奖励可预测部分的互补性“思维盈余”，以减少与结果级监督的重叠。

Result: 在三个模型家族的五个模型上进行实验，TCR在多样化的基准测试中一致提升了模型的对齐性能；消融实验验证了EMA残差公式和样本特定清单监督的重要性。

Conclusion: 过程导向的奖励机制（如TCR）能有效补充结果级监督，显著提升大语言模型在人类偏好对齐方面的表现。

Abstract: LLM preference alignment aims to optimize models toward human preferences across diverse user instructions. Reinforcement learning has become a major post-training approach for this goal, but existing proxy rewards are often outcome-level, mainly evaluating the final response while providing limited guidance for the reasoning trajectory. This can make credit assignment coarse when multiple responses receive similar final scores, leaving trajectory-level preferences under-specified. To address this limitation, we propose Thinking Checklist Reward (TCR), a process-oriented reward for RL-based preference alignment. TCR converts preference pairs into sample-specific thinking checklists and uses them to evaluate whether the generated reasoning trace addresses the preference-implied considerations. To reduce overlap with outcome-level supervision, TCR further introduces an exponential moving average (EMA) residual formulation to isolate a complementary thinking surplus beyond what is predictable from the outcome reward. Experiments on five models from three model families show that TCR consistently improves alignment performance across diverse benchmarks, with ablations further validating the importance of EMA-based residual formulation and sample-specific checklist supervision.

</details>


### [150] [Know Your Agent: Reconnaissance-Driven Pentesting of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19837)
*Or Zion Eliav,Eyal Lenga,Shir Bernstien,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KYA框架，通过自动化黑盒侦察发现AI代理弱点，并利用提取的知识资产构建更强大的间接提示注入攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统渗透测试依赖各阶段的侦察以发现未知弱点并强化攻击；作者认为AI代理同样需要这种处理，但目前缺乏对代理侦察的形式化定义和系统化方法。

Method: 形式化代理侦察过程，识别其试图提取的知识资产（内容、用途及被利用的代理弱点）；开发Know Your Agent (KYA)框架，实现自动化的黑盒、基于侦察的渗透测试，包括探测代理、构建目标画像并利用画像设计更强攻击。

Result: 在代理安全基准测试和真实世界编码代理上评估了KYA的有效性；公开了KYA框架、相关基准测试及基线实现代码，以促进可复现性研究。

Conclusion: 将传统渗透测试中的侦察理念引入AI代理安全领域是必要且有效的，KYA框架为自动化评估代理安全性提供了新的方法论工具和资源。

Abstract: Traditional pentesting uses reconnaissance at each step to uncover unseen weaknesses, build stronger attacks, and advance the objective; we argue that AI agents require the same treatment. We formalize agent reconnaissance by modeling the process and identifying the knowledge assets it seeks to extract: what they are, how they are used, and which agent weaknesses they exploit to give adversaries leverage in indirect prompt injection attacks. We instantiate these insights in Know Your Agent (KYA), a framework that automates black-box, reconnaissance-driven pentesting by probing agents, building target profiles, and using those profiles to craft stronger attacks. We evaluate KYA on agent-security benchmarks and a real-world coding agent, and release KYA, its benchmarks, and baseline implementations for reproducibility.

</details>


### [151] [DocOps: A Verifiable Benchmark for Autonomous Agents in Complex Document Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19865)
*Jiazhen Jiang,Boxi Cao,Lingyong Yan,Yaojie Lu,Hongyu Lin,Shuaiqiang Wang,Dawei Yin,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DocOps，一个基于层级分类法的文档操作评估框架。通过对多种主流模型在复杂工作流中的系统评估，揭示了当前智能体在处理长程、高耦合任务时的局限性，并识别出状态追踪崩溃、语义验证浅层化和结构元数据破坏性编辑三大失败模式，旨在为未来鲁棒性数字生态系统的智能体设计提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着自主代理（autonomous agents）的快速发展，其可靠地操纵通用数字文档的能力对于实现通用AI助手和自动化复杂工作流至关重要。然而，目前缺乏一种确定性的评估框架来全面衡量这一能力。

Method: 引入DocOps框架，该框架受现实世界实践启发，通过分层分类法将文档操作解构为原子维度和递增的工作流复杂度。基于此框架，对代表性闭源和开源模型在各种智能体工具链（agentic harnesses）中进行了系统性评估，并对现有代理的操作行为进行了细粒度分析。

Result: 1. 即使是最先进的前沿配置，在处理高度耦合、长程任务时仍表现出深刻的局限性。2. 识别出三个关键失败模式：长期状态追踪崩溃、浅层语义验证、以及对结构元数据的破坏性编辑。3. 暴露了智能体在维持全局文档一致性方面的能力边界。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前智能体在复杂数字生态系统中的能力局限，强调了设计具备非破坏性特征、能保持全局一致性的鲁棒智能体的重要性，为未来的系统设计指明了方向。

Abstract: As autonomous agents rapidly evolve, their ability to reliably manipulate ubiquitous digital documents has become critical for enabling general-purpose AI assistants and automating complex workspace workflows. In this paper, we introduce DocOps, a deterministically verifiable evaluation framework underpinned by a hierarchical taxonomy that deconstructs document operations inspired by real-world practices into atomic dimensions and escalating workflow complexities. Based on DocOps, we systematically evaluate representative closed- and open-source models across various agentic harnesses, revealing that even the most advanced frontier configurations still exhibit profound limitations when handling highly coupled, long-range tasks. Furthermore, a fine-grained analysis of existing agents' manipulation behaviors uncovers 3 key failure modes: long-term state tracking collapse, shallow semantic verification, and destructive editing of structural metadata. Ultimately, our work exposes the capability boundaries of agents in maintaining global document consistency, shedding light on the future design of robust, non-destructive agents for complex digital ecosystems.

</details>


### [152] [JANUS: Foreseeing Latent Risk for Long-Horizon Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19913)
*Yuan Xiong,Linji Hao,Shizhu He,Yequan Wang,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Janus框架和Vanguard模型，通过多智能体模拟生成轨迹，利用联合优化的预期与裁决任务，在工具使用代理执行前预测并阻断延迟安全风险，显著提升安全保护率且未降低良性任务完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统代理安全侧重于内容审核，需转向在工具使用代理行动之前预防操作性故障，特别是针对长周期代理中存在的延迟风险。

Method: 构建Janus框架，通过多智能体模拟合成多样化代理轨迹；训练共享策略包含两个耦合任务：预期任务（预测与安全相关的未来状态）和裁决任务（基于观察前缀和预期未来进行安全判定）；采用CoAA-RL联合优化，根据下游安全判断的效用奖励预测结果。

Result: 生成的守卫模型Vanguard在四个代理安全基准测试中，平均保护能力较基线守卫提升15.9个百分点，同时良性任务完成率提高5.1个百分点。

Conclusion: Janus框架能有效实现前瞻性代理安全，通过预判潜在风险在执行前阻断不安全行为，兼顾了安全性与任务完成效率。

Abstract: Agent safety is moving from content moderation toward preventing operational failures before tool-using agents act. We propose Janus, a foresight-oriented framework for long-horizon agent safety that trains guards to anticipate delayed risks from partial trajectories. Janus synthesizes diverse agent trajectories via multi-agent simulation and learns a shared policy with two coupled tasks: an anticipation task that forecasts safety-relevant futures and an adjudication task that decides safety from both the observed prefix and anticipated future. The two tasks are jointly optimized with CoAA-RL, which rewards forecasts by their utility for downstream safety judgment. The resulting guard model, Vanguard, blocks unsafe actions before execution. Across four agent-safety benchmarks, Vanguard improves average protection by 15.9 percentage points over baseline guards while increasing benign task completion by 5.1 percentage points.

</details>


### [153] [Long-Term Sequential Decision Making under Risk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19914)
*Irmaan,Mirzanejad,Nadjet Bourdache,Abdel-Illah Mouaddib*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ERQDP方法，通过无枚举、无采样的精确动态规划求解基于根的风险分析目标，提供带误差界保证的优化解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限视界MDP中的基于根的风险目标（应用秩依赖泛函）是非线性的，破坏了贝尔曼最优性，导致直接优化不可行。

Method: 提出ERQDP算法：1) 通过精确DP求解秩-量词代理问题；2) 在离散化回报网格上利用概率质量函数(PMF)精确评估策略；3) 在任何时间循环中细化代理并报告显式上下界间隙证书。

Result: ERQDP能返回带认证解或显式残差间隙的策略，支持快速风险参数扫描，显著降低运行时耗，同时兼容风险厌恶和风险寻求行为。

Conclusion: ERQDP提供了一种高效、可证明的框架来解决破坏贝尔曼最优性的非线性风险MDP规划问题。

Abstract: We study finite-horizon MDP planning under \emph{root-based} (resolute) risk objectives that apply a rank-dependent functional to the distribution of total returns. Such objectives are non-linear in the return distribution and generally break Bellman optimality, so direct optimization by scenario-tree enumeration is intractable. We propose \textbf{ERQDP}, an enumeration-free and sampling-free method that solves a rank--quantile surrogate via exact DP (Dynamic Programming), evaluates candidate policies exactly by DP over return Probability Mass Functions (PMFs) on a discretized return grid (with an explicit rounding bound), and refines the surrogate in an anytime loop that reports an explicit upper--lower gap (certificate) for the target objective up to discretization budgets. Across tested benchmarks, ERQDP returns certified solutions or explicit residual gaps, enables fast risk-parameter sweeps with substantial runtime gains, and supports both risk-averse and risk-seeking behaviors.

</details>


### [154] [MOF-Sleuth: Tool-Grounded Reward Alignment for Explainable Fine-Grained MOF CIF Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19935)
*Yu Liu,Zhiwei Yang,Diandian Guo,Kun Peng,Fangfang Yuan,Cong Cao,Chaozhuo Li,Zhiyuan Ma,Yanbing Liu,Guobin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MOF-Sleuth，一种结合确定性证据提取与强化学习推理的LLM代理，用于高精度、可解释的金属有机框架（MOF）晶体学信息文件（CIF）审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MOF数据库中的细微化学/结构错误影响下游模拟；现有方法缺乏基于证据的解释能力，且直接利用LLM进行审计成本高、可靠性低，因化学证据隐含且需复杂计算。

Method: 构建包含“法医实验室”（提取组成、几何、连接性等硬性证据）和“侦探推理引擎”（基于证据生成解释）的双模块架构；采用奖励引导的强化学习（RL），不仅优化最终诊断结果，还奖励引用的化学证据及证据支持的诊断过程。

Result: 在四个基准测试中建立SOTA性能，显著提升检测准确率、归因能力及基于证据的解释质量；引入Chem-GD指标验证诊断的事实相关性。

Conclusion: 通过强化耦合化学证据与语言模型解释，MOF-Sleuth实现了从粗粒度筛查到细粒度、可解释诊断的突破，为MOF数据质量控制提供新范式。

Abstract: Large metal-organic framework (MOF) databases support simulation, screening, and machine learning through crystallographic information files (CIFs). Subtle chemical and structural errors in these inputs can compromise downstream results and hinder manual inspection. LLM advances in computational chemistry offer paths beyond predictive screening toward fine-grained diagnosis with evidence-grounded explanations. However, two challenges remain: (i) limited fine-grained attribution: MOF-specific validators and machine-learning models scale detection but provide fixed checks, readiness scores, or coarse labels rather than evidence-grounded explanations; and (ii) unreliable CIF reasoning: direct LLM auditing is costly and unreliable because chemical evidence is implicit across atom-site records and requires geometric, connectivity, occupancy, and charge calculations. Both stem from weak coupling between chemical evidence and language-model explanation. We introduce MOF-Sleuth, a reinforcement-guided CIF auditing agent with two modules: a deterministic Forensic Lab and a Sleuth reasoning engine. The Lab derives composition, geometry, connectivity, occupancy, coordination, and charge evidence, and Sleuth uses this evidence to produce an evidence-grounded explanation, error types, and a binary decision. Reward-guided reinforcement learning (RL) turns tool measurements into chemical explanation-level supervision, rewarding not only the final answer but also cited chemical evidence and evidence-supported diagnoses. We introduce Chemically Grounded Diagnosis (Chem-GD), a metric that assesses whether a correct diagnosis is explained by factual, relevant CIF-derived evidence. Across four benchmarks, MOF-Sleuth establishes state-of-the-art performance among LLM-based approaches and MOF-specific machine-learning methods, demonstrating gains in detection, attribution, and grounded explanation quality.

</details>


### [155] [SenWorld: A Digital-Twin Simulation for Generating Context-Rich Evaluation Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19949)
*Zenghui Zhou,Xiaoyang Li,Xiaoxuan Qiao,Zhilang Wei,Tianming Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SenWorld，一种基于真实数据的确定性数字孪生模拟器，用于生成带确切答案的智能手机助手评估数据，解决隐私敏感和标注困难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能手机助手需处理纵向个人数据，但现有评估缺乏上下文丰富的已知正确答案数据，且真实设备轨迹因隐私问题难以共享。

Method: 构建SenWorld数字孪生仿真系统，利用真实地图、天气等数据生成16个北京人物角色的全天行为；通过全系统快照存档信号，并以指向现有记录的指针而非LLM标注来标记评估案例。

Result: 生成数据在类别分布（JSD 0.070）和通信日常节奏（JSD < 0.1）上接近真实用户基准；揭示了某生产环境手机助手的78个检索错误，主要集中在通话和短信记录，且确认为助手端错误。

Conclusion: SenWorld提供了一种隐私安全、可复现且经过分布验证的路径，用于生成标签由构造过程确定的评估数据。

Abstract: Smartphone personal assistants reason over longitudinal personal data, yet evaluating them requires context-rich evaluation data whose correct answers are known, and real device traces are too privacy-sensitive to share. To address this challenge, we present SenWorld, a physically grounded, deterministic, event-sourced digital-twin simulation that generates such data with ground truth fixed by construction. In SenWorld, personas live through a full day in a world built from real map, weather, holiday, and network data; every observable signal is archived in full-system snapshots; and each evaluation case is labeled by a pointer to an existing record rather than by post-hoc annotation or a large language model (LLM) judge. We evaluate this method with 16 personas in Beijing. The generated data closely matches the held-out real-user benchmark in category distribution (Jensen--Shannon divergence (JSD) 0.070) and in the daily rhythm of communication records (JSD below 0.1), though generated records remain shorter than real ones. Without scripted interaction, personas form a fully reciprocated dialogue subgraph and differentiated behavioral repertoires. Projected into 717 evaluation cases, the generated data exposes 78 failures in a production smartphone assistant, concentrating on call and Short Message Service (SMS) records while contacts, schedules, and alarms never fail. The snapshot pointer confirms each failure as an assistant-side retrieval error, with no LLM judge involved. Overall, SenWorld offers a privacy-safe, reproducible, and distribution-checked path to evaluation data whose labels are fixed by construction.

</details>


### [156] [EvoThink: Evolving Thinking in Large Reasoning Models via Self-Pruning and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19962)
*Xinbang Dai,Zheyu Xin,Huikang Hu,Lin Ren,Rihui Jin,Guohui Xiao,Guilin Qi,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Yuyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoThink框架，通过自剪枝训练(SPT)和顿悟时刻偏好优化(AMPO)，在减少冗余验证步骤的同时提升大型推理模型(LRMs)的推理效率与能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缓解LRM过度思考（如快速-慢速思维切换、轨迹压缩）的方法缺乏对有益与冗余步骤的细粒度区分，可能损害推理能力以换取效率。

Method: 1. 自剪枝训练(SPT)：无监督方法，迭代剪除冗余推理步骤并基于精简轨迹进行自训练；2. 顿悟时刻偏好优化(AMPO)：受遗传算法启发，识别有价值的失败尝试，合成“从错到对”的顿悟数据，优化模型内化该推理模式。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准测试中，EvoThink显著减少了推理时的token使用量，同时提升了LRMs的推理能力。

Conclusion: EvoThink能有效平衡推理效率与能力，通过减少冗余和鼓励新路径探索解决过度思考问题。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often suffer from overthinking due to redundant verification steps. Existing approaches for mitigating overthinking, such as fast-slow thinking switching and reasoning trajectory compression, fail to make a fine-grained distinction between beneficial and redundant steps within the LRM's reasoning process, and may thus impair reasoning capability in their pursuit of efficiency. To simultaneously improve reasoning efficiency and capability, we propose EvoThink, a framework that reduces redundant verification and encourages the exploration of new reasoning paths. EvoThink comprises two key components: Self-Pruning Training (SPT), an unsupervised method that iteratively prunes redundant reasoning steps and self-trains on the concise trajectories; and Aha-Moment Preference Optimization (AMPO), which, inspired by genetic algorithms, identifies valuable failed reasoning attempts, synthesizes from-wrong-to-right aha-moment data, and optimizes the model to internalize this reasoning pattern. Extensive evaluations across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks demonstrate that EvoThink not only substantially reduces inference-time token usage but also improves the reasoning capability of LRMs.

</details>


### [157] [The Giant Hippocampus: From Structural Monoculture to a System of Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19973)
*Jaeho Seol*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批评了当前AI领域过度依赖统一Transformer架构的现象，指出这与神经科学中大脑皮层具有异质性结构的证据相悖。文章主张放弃“硬件彩票”式的单一模板缩放策略，转而采用“异构拓扑网络”，即在设计阶段就根据计算需求指定模块化结构，以实现更高效的认知功能实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI研究者使用的统一Transformer模型与神经科学家观察到的大脑皮层异质性结构之间的巨大差距；纠正将Transformer视为通用皮层替代品的结构性错误，避免将其仅作为海马体类比物应用于不适宜的任务（如听觉、执行门控、工作记忆）。

Method: 1. 历史回顾：引用一个世纪以来从Brodmann到单细胞Patch-seq的细胞架构研究，证明不同认知功能由定性不同的结构实现。
2. 案例分析：以卷积神经网络（CNN）为例，说明局部感受野和层级深度如何编码归纳偏置，从而在较少数据下实现强图像识别。
3. 批判性分析：揭示“硬件彩票”导致Transformer成为阻力最小的路径而非原则性选择；指出Mixture-of-Experts并未真正提供多样性，仅是参数分区。
4. 功能主义分析：论证Transformer本质上是海马体的功能模拟，而非通用皮层。
5. 提出替代方案：设计一种“异构拓扑网络”（Heterogeneous Topological Network），作为系统之系统，通过标准化接口通信，并在训练前指定模块化以保持各自的归纳偏置。

Result: 证明了当前AI架构存在结构性缺陷，即错误地将适用于特定功能（如海马体相关的记忆整合）的统一架构推广至所有任务；确立了CNN作为正确利用结构先验的历史证据；提出了基于结构证据进行前置模块化设计的新型AI架构范式。

Conclusion: AI架构设计应遵循“设计纪律”，即在训练前根据结构证据指定模块性，而不是从已训练模型的行为中逆向工程架构。未来的AI应发展为异构拓扑网络，其中不同模块保留其所需的归纳偏置，并通过标准化接口通信，从而更有效地模拟生物智能的多样性与效率。

Abstract: AI researchers describe state-of-the-art models as one thing repeated at scale: the Transformer, wired identically for text, pixels, or speech. Neuroscientists describe the cortex as a mosaic - dense Layer 4 in visual cortex for spatial encoding, thick Layers 5/6 in motion cortex for temporal integration - different jobs solved by different structures. This paper argues the gap is a structural error, not a stylistic one, and is measurable. A century of cytoarchitecture, from Brodmann to single-cell Patch-seq, shows distinct cognitive functions are implemented by qualitatively different structures, not by rescaling one template. The convolutional neural network is the field's own proof: local receptive fields and hierarchical depth encoded this prior directly, reaching strong image recognition on far less data than later architectures needed. The paper traces how this lesson was discarded: the "Hardware Lottery" made the Transformer the path of least resistance, not the principled choice, and Mixture-of-Experts, often cited as diversity, in fact partitions parameters among identical experts. A functionalist analysis shows the Transformer is best understood as a functional analog of the hippocampal formation, not a general-purpose cortex - the same mistake as treating cortex as one giant Broca's area, except the field has now standardized on a giant hippocampus, applied to tasks it was never built for: audition, executive gating, working memory. The paper closes with an alternative: a Heterogeneous Topological Network, a System of Systems in which distinct modules keep the inductive bias their computation demands and communicate through standardized interfaces. This is a design discipline for AI architects, not cognitive science: specify modularity before training, using structural evidence as a design input rather than reverse-engineering architecture from a trained model's behavior.

</details>


### [158] [Coordinating from Memory: Graph-Structured Experience Reuse for Multi-Agent Adaptation in Dynamic Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19985)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhanhui Lin,Zhaokun Yan,Youyang Ni,Chenjun Lei,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GSEM框架，利用图结构经验记忆解决动态制造环境中的多智能体协调问题，通过检索相似历史 episode 加速策略适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法将每次扰动视为独立事件，浪费了可加速未来适应的宝贵协调经验；动态制造环境中频繁扰动（如机器故障、紧急订单）需要更高效的自适应机制。

Method: 构建图结构化经验记忆（GSEM）框架：1. 将历史协调过程编码为异构关系图，捕捉任务依赖、机器状态及智能体协作模式；2. 基于图神经网络（GNN）的检索机制，在新扰动发生时识别结构相似的历史片段；3. 利用检索到的经验指导策略调整，而非从零开始学习。

Result: 在三种扰动类型的动态柔性作业车间调度基准测试中，GSEM相比最强基线模型：1. 完工时间（makespan）减少4.1%-10.0%；2. 适应时间缩短33%-38%；3. 在更高扰动频率下优势更显著；消融实验验证了图结构编码和相似度检索的必要性；跨扰动迁移实验证明了所学协作模式的泛化能力。

Conclusion: GSEM框架通过结构化存储和检索历史协调经验，有效提升了多智能体系统在动态制造环境中的适应效率和性能，且具备良好的跨场景泛化性。

Abstract: Dynamic manufacturing environments require multi-agent systems to coordinate effectively under frequent operational disturbances such as machine failures, urgent job arrivals, and processing time variations. Existing multi-agent reinforcement learning approaches treat each disturbance episode independently, discarding valuable coordination experience that could accelerate future adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Graph-Structured Experiential Memory (GSEM) framework for multi-agent coordination in dynamic manufacturing. The framework encodes historical coordination episodes as heterogeneous relational graphs that capture task dependencies, machine states, and inter-agent collaboration patterns. When a new disturbance occurs, a graph neural network-based retrieval mechanism identifies structurally similar past episodes, enabling experience-guided policy adaptation rather than learning from scratch. Experiments on dynamic flexible job-shop scheduling benchmarks with three disturbance types show that GSEM reduces makespan by 4.1%-10.0% and adaptation time by 33%-38% compared to the strongest memory-augmented baseline, with the advantage increasing under higher disturbance frequency. Ablation studies and cross-disturbance transfer experiments further validate the necessity of graph-structured encoding and similarity-based retrieval and demonstrate the cross-disturbance generalizability of learned coordination patterns.

</details>


### [159] [CLARK: Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19996)
*Yousef Khan,Luca Gherardini,Marco Maratea,Joel Arrais,Jose Sousa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CLARK框架，结合知识图谱、符号规则挖掘和概率推理（LP^MLN），解决机器学习在数据分布变化下的性能下降及先验知识整合不足问题，提升分类任务的泛化性和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习模型在处理数据分布变化时性能下降，且难以有效整合先验知识，无法适应不确定和动态变化的信息环境。

Method: 构建CLARK框架：基于CACTUS生成的知识图谱，将其结构转化为LP^MLN程序；利用符号学习者提出候选规则，并通过概率权重学习进行校准，实现不确定性下的推理和图结构优化。

Result: 在两个医学数据集上的评估显示，CLARK显著提升了分类性能和推理的泛化能力，同时保持了规则的可解释性。

Conclusion: CLARK提供了一种原则性的方法，用于构建自适应、可解释且以知识驱动的分类模型，有效应对数据分布漂移和不确定性挑战。

Abstract: Machine Learning models are widely used for automating classification tasks by extracting statistical patterns from data. However, their performance deteriorates if the data distribution changes, making them ill-suited to handle uncertain and evolving information. Moreover, they provide limited support for integrating prior knowledge. To address these limitations, we present CLARK (Closed-loop Learning for Adaptive Reasoning over Knowledge Graphs), a framework that integrates knowledge graphs, symbolic rule mining, and probabilistic reasoning under the Logic Programs with Markov Logic Networks (LP$^{\text{MLN}}$) formalism. Starting from CACTUS-derived KGs, CLARK translates graph structure into an LP$^{\text{MLN}}$ program and iteratively enriches it with candidate rules proposed by symbolic learners. These rules are calibrated through probabilistic weight learning, enabling reasoning under uncertainty and refinement of the underlying graph structure. We evaluate CLARK on two medical datasets, analysing both rule quality and downstream classification performance. Results demonstrate that CLARK leads to improved classification performance and more generalisable inference. Overall, CLARK provides a principled approach to constructing adaptive, interpretable, knowledge-driven models for classification.

</details>


### [160] [Safe Remediation as Risk-Constrained Intervention Decision in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20005)
*Chengxiao Dai,Zhaokun Yan,Chenjun Lei,Qiao Li,Luyan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）的安全自动化修复框架，通过分解风险维度并设计上下文自适应的人机协作门控机制，在降低误修率的同时提升修复成功率并减轻运维负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代IT运维中错误修复的成本极高，但现有的自动化修复系统仅关注生成操作而非判断是否需要干预，将安全性视为事后手动审批的附属品，缺乏对安全性的系统性保障。

Method: 1. 将安全修复重构为风险受限的干预决策问题，建模为CMDP，在限制误修率（FRR）的前提下最大化修复成功率；2. 引入包含爆炸半径、可逆性和认知不确定性的三维风险分解模型，提供可解释的安全接口；3. 设计上下文自适应的人在人环（HITL）门控机制，根据值班负载和业务关键性动态调整升级策略。策略通过历史事件日志离线学习。

Result: 在Train Ticket微服务基准测试和Chaos Mesh故障注入实验中，该框架相比强基线方案将误修率降低了39%，修复成功率提升了2.5分，并比固定阈值变体减少了17%的值班升级负载。

Conclusion: 通过将安全修复形式化为带约束的优化问题并结合可解释的风险分解与自适应人机协作机制，可以有效平衡自动化效率与操作安全性，显著降低误操作风险并优化运维资源分配。

Abstract: In modern IT operations (IT-Ops), the cost of an incorrect repair often exceeds the cost of no action at all. Yet existing automated remediation systems are designed to generate actions rather than to decide whether intervention is warranted, leaving safety as an afterthought enforced by manual approval. This paper makes three contributions to close this gap: (i) we reformulate safe remediation as a risk-constrained intervention decision problem and cast it as a Constrained Markov Decision Process (CMDP), in which the agent maximizes repair success subject to a bounded false remediation rate (FRR); (ii) we introduce a three-dimensional risk decomposition comprising blast radius, reversibility, and epistemic uncertainty, providing operators with an interpretable per-action safety interface; and (iii) we design a context-adaptive human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate that turns escalation from a binary failsafe into a bandwidth-aware control layer responsive to on-call load and business criticality. The full policy is learned offline from historical incident logs, enabling explicit control of the expected FRR. Experiments on the Train Ticket microservice benchmark with Chaos Mesh fault injection and an RCAEval-aligned fault taxonomy show that our framework reduces FRR by 39% while improving repair success by 2.5 points over a strong runbook baseline, and reduces on-call escalation load by 17% relative to a fixed-threshold variant.

</details>


### [161] [EvoDRC: A Self-Evolving Agentic Framework for Automated DRC Violation Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20019)
*Bing-Yue Wu,Chia-Tung Ho,Haoyu Yang,Brucek Khailany,Vidya A. Chhabria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出EvoDRC，一种基于技能进化的智能体框架，用于自动化高级节点物理设计中的DRC修复。通过从参考设计蒸馏知识并持续进化技能，结合LLM代理和反馈工具，显著减少残留设计规则违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决先进节点物理设计中DRC闭合的主要瓶颈，消除对人工工程变更订单（ECO）迭代的依赖，克服修复过程中复杂几何交互、保持电路连通性和避免新违规的挑战。

Method: 1. 使用来自无关参考设计的知识初始化层特定修复技能；2. 从目标设计收集的可追溯修复经验持续进化这些技能；3. 将布局分解为有界修复区域，并为每个区域分配LLM修复代理；4. 利用局部DRC分析、连通性检查和影响预览工具提供修改反馈；5. 将修复操作及结果存储在知识库中以驱动技能进化。

Result: 在DAC26 DRC基准测试的七个块级设计上实验表明，EvoDRC相比报告基线实现了73.5%的整体减少（指DRVs或相关指标）。

Conclusion: EvoDRC作为一种技能进化框架，能有效自动化块级DRC修复过程，显著提升效率并减少手动干预需求。

Abstract: Design rule check (DRC) closure remains a major bottleneck in advanced-node physical design. Although detailed routers are rule-aware, residual design rule violations (DRVs) often require manual engineering change order iterations. Automating this process is challenging because repairs must account for complex geometric interactions, preserve circuit connectivity, and avoid introducing new violations. We present EvoDRC, a skill-evolution framework for agentic block-level DRC repair. EvoDRC initializes layer-specific repair skills using knowledge distilled from an unrelated reference design and continuously evolves these skills using traceable repair experience collected from the target design. EvoDRC decomposes the layout into bounded repair regions and assigns an LLM repair agent to each region. Local DRC analysis, connectivity-checking, and impact-preview tools provide feedback on proposed modifications. Repair operations and their resulting DRV changes are stored in a knowledge database and used to evolve the repair skills. Experiments on seven block-level designs from the DAC26 DRC Benchmark show that EvoDRC achieves a 73.5\% overall reduction compared to the reported baseline.

</details>


### [162] [Global Difference Constraint Propagation for Constraint Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20022)
*Lucas Kletzander,Jip J. Dekker,Andreas Schutt,Peter J. Stuckey*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对差异约束（Difference Constraints）的全局传播器，通过同时处理所有约束而非单独处理，显著提高了求解效率，并实现了在惰性子句生成求解器中的解释功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的有限域传播算法通常将每个差异约束视为独立的传播器，导致传播过程虽然完备但效率低下；尽管SAT modulo theory求解器已包含相关理论求解器，但其需求与全局传播器不同，且缺乏有效的解释机制以集成到现代求解框架中。

Method: 构建一个基于边界一致性的全局传播器来同时处理差异约束；分析其与理论求解器的区别，特别是如何为传播操作提供解释（explanations），以便将其嵌入到惰性子句生成（lazy clause generation）求解器中。

Result: 实验表明，采用全局方式处理差异约束相比标准的独立传播方法，能显著提升求解性能。

Conclusion: 将差异约束作为整体进行全局传播，并结合解释机制集成到惰性子句生成求解器中，是一种比传统独立传播更高效、更可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Difference constraints of the form $x - y \leq d$ are well studied, with efficient algorithms for satisfaction and implication, because of their connection to shortest paths. Finite domain propagation algorithms, however, typically do not make use of these algorithms, and treat each difference constraint as a separate propagator. Propagation does guarantee completeness of solving, but can be needlessly slow. In this paper we describe how to build a (bounds consistent) global propagator for difference constraints that treats them all simultaneously. SAT modulo theory solvers have included theory solvers for difference constraints for some time. While a theory solver for difference constraints gives the basis of a global difference constraint propagator, we show how the requirements on the propagator are quite different. Crucially, we show how to explain propagations by a global difference constraint propagator, in order to use it within a lazy clause generation solver. We give experiments showing that treating difference constraints globally can substantially improve on the standard propagation approach.

</details>


### [163] [Reading and Steering Representations of Materials-Science Mechanisms in an Open-Weight Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20058)
*Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现Google Gemma模型中材料科学机制信息以三种可分离形式存在：概念可读、状态变换携带方向性、内部表征因果控制答案。通过因果干预证明物理关系在状态变换中比绝对状态更显著，而非仅由数值比较解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型能回答科学问题，但正确输出无法揭示模型是否真正理解或使用了底层物理规律。本研究旨在验证并量化模型内部表示中物理机制信息的存在形式及其因果作用。

Method: 研究结合直接词汇读出与雅可比（Jacobian）词汇读出、无选项状态几何分析、60条定律的反事实基准测试及因果干预实验。使用Gemma-4-E4B-it模型，通过隐藏状态分析、定向定律测试和双向干预来探测物理信息的编码方式。

Result: 1. 概念可读性：3个独立拟合的雅可比透镜复现了概念排名，盲测成功识别9/10机制家族。2. 状态几何局限性：72提示基准显示隐藏状态邻域看似有物理组织，但图审计表明这也可由数值比较完全解释。3. 因果控制有效性：在反向物理输入提示中，状态变换正确排序了60条冻结关系中的39条定向定律，词法对照接近随机水平。4. 干预效果：双向干预使答案概率向物理适当结果偏移，反事实状态补丁在不同机制间转移了对立决策信号。

Conclusion: 材料科学机制信息在模型中以概念、受控状态变换方向和因果控制三种形式存在。物理关系在受控的状态变化中比在绝对状态中更为显著，证明了模型内部确实编码了可被因果干预提取的物理规律，而不仅仅是统计相关性或数值近似。

Abstract: Large language models can answer scientific questions, yet a correct output does not reveal whether the model represents or uses the governing physics. Here we show that materials science mechanism information in the open-weight google/gemma-4-E4B-it model has three experimentally separable forms: concepts are readable in individual hidden states, constitutive orientation is carried by controlled transformations between states, and selected internal representations causally control engineering answers. We combine matched direct and Jacobian vocabulary readouts, option-free state geometry, a 60-law counterfactual benchmark and causal interventions. In 50 held-out materials descriptions, three independently fitted Jacobian lenses reproduced concept ranks, and target-free word sets from both readouts enabled blinded identification of 9 of 10 mechanism families. A separate 72-prompt benchmark produced mechanism-specific hidden-state neighborhoods, but an exact graph audit showed that this apparent physical organization was equally explained by numerical comparison. We therefore compared otherwise identical prompts in which only the direction of the physical input was reversed, asking whether the resulting hidden-state movement followed the supplied constitutive law. These state transformations ordered direct, physically neutral and inverse laws across 60 frozen relations and correctly oriented 39 of 40 directional laws, whereas lexical controls were near chance. Bidirectional interventions shifted answer probabilities toward or away from the physically appropriate outcome across all 12 matched cases, while counterfactual state patches transferred opposing decision signals across mechanisms and answer formats. Physical relationships were therefore more visible in controlled state changes than in absolute states alone.

</details>


### [164] [PRO-LONG: Programmatic Memory Enables Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20064)
*Alexis Fox,Junlin Wang,Paul Rosu,Bhuwan Dhingra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PRO-LONG框架，通过程序化记忆和结构化交互日志解决LLM智能体在长视距任务中的上下文管理难题，显著提升ARC-AGI-3表现并大幅降低Token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）智能体在需要持续感知、推理和探索的长视距任务中表现不佳，特别是在ARC-AGI-3等持续学习基准测试中。现有的上下文管理方法面临“保留更多信息导致检索相关细节变得不可行”的重大权衡困境。

Method: 提出PRO-LONG，一个面向长视距探索性设置的极简上下文管理框架。该方法围绕程序化记忆构建，保持完整、结构化的交互日志，并利用编程智能体的最新进展高效搜索历史数据，以解决信息保留与检索可行性之间的权衡问题。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3公共游戏集上，PRO-LONG相比基础编程智能体平均提升18.0个百分点；在前沿模型上达到或超过最先进的专用智能体框架（最高pass@1为76.1%），同时Token使用量减少4.2-5.8倍。结合Fable 5模型，实现97.4%的best@2准确率，总成本仅为1,750美元。

Conclusion: PRO-LONG通过结构化的程序化记忆机制，有效解决了LLM智能体在长序列观察下的上下文管理瓶颈，在提升性能的同时显著降低了计算成本，为长视距任务提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks require sustained perception, reasoning, and exploration, and are a persistent challenge for large language model (LLM) agents. This gap is reflected in their limited performance on continual learning benchmarks such as ARC-AGI-3, especially when models are evaluated out of the box. Various agent harnesses have been proposed to close this gap, and each commits to a strategy for handling long sequences of observations, i.e., what information to save from the environment and how to load it into model context, a choice we argue is particularly consequential. Existing methods for context management face a significant tradeoff, as preserving more information makes retrieving relevant details less tractable. We propose PRO-LONG, a minimal context management framework built around programmatic memory for LLM agents in long-horizon, exploratory settings. PRO-LONG addresses the tradeoff by keeping a complete, structured interaction log and capitalizing on recent progress in coding agents to search this history efficiently. On the full ARC-AGI-3 public game set, PRO-LONG improves over a base coding agent by an average of 18.0 percentage points across frontier models, and matches or exceeds state-of-the-art specialized harnesses (up to 76.1% pass@1) while using 4.2-5.8x fewer tokens. With Fable 5, PRO-LONG achieves 97.4% best@2 at a total cost of \$1,750. Relevant code and logs are available at https://github.com/alexisfox7/PRO-LONG.

</details>


### [165] [TRUST-ESD: A Risk-Calibrated and Governance-Aware AI Framework for Enterprise Strategic Decision Support Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20065)
*Tian Qiu,Li Yan,Mahabubur Rahman Miraj,Shanqin Yi,Md Intekhab Rahman Galib,Jahid Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TRUST-ESD框架，旨在通过结合不确定性校准、风险计量和合规性治理，解决企业战略决策支持中缺乏风险意识和可解释性的问题，显著提升了决策的风险调整后效用和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的企业战略决策支持系统往往仅关注预测准确性，而忽视了不确定性感知、风险校准、可解释性及治理合规性，导致在不确定环境下做出的决策可能面临高风险且缺乏信任基础。

Method: 提出TRUST-ESD框架，该方法通过预测效用估计评估可行的反事实策略，并结合共形不确定性校准、基于CVaR的下行风险评分、风险记忆检索、代码化政策治理、可解释性机制及人工监督，以平衡价值、可靠性、风险暴露和合规性。

Result: 实验表明，与强基线相比，TRUST-ESD将风险调整效用提高了7.95%，风险暴露降低了23.22%，CVaR降低了23.78%，校准误差降低了13.89%，解释保真度提高了10.90%，治理合规性提高了9.76%，同时保持了具有竞争力的预测准确性。

Conclusion: 不确定性校准、下行风险评分、风险记忆、可解释性和治理验证共同作用，显著提升了企业决策的可信度和鲁棒性，证明了该框架在复杂商业环境下的有效性。

Abstract: Enterprise strategic decision support requires AI systems that are not only accurate, but also uncertainty-aware, risk-calibrated, explainable, and governance-compliant. This paper proposes TRUST-ESD, a risk-calibrated and governance-aware framework for enterprise decision support under uncertainty. TRUST-ESD evaluates feasible counterfactual strategies through predictive utility estimation, conformal uncertainty calibration, CVaR-based downside-risk scoring, risk-memory retrieval, policy-as-code governance, explainability, and human oversight. Unlike prediction-only methods that select actions by maximum expected utility, TRUST-ESD recommends strategies that balance value, reliability, risk exposure, and compliance. Experimental results show that TRUST-ESD improves risk-adjusted utility by 7.95%, reduces risk exposure by 23.22%, reduces CVaR by 23.78%, lowers calibration error by 13.89%, improves explanation fidelity by 10.90%, and increases governance compliance by 9.76% compared with strong uncertainty-aware baselines, while maintaining competitive predictive accuracy. Ablation and case-study analyses further confirm that uncertainty calibration, downside-risk scoring, risk memory, explainability, and governance validation jointly improve trustworthy enterprise decision-making.

</details>


### [166] [CUSUM-Shaped Inference-Time Monitoring and Targeted Re-Decoding for Quantized Small Language Model Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20129)
*El Hassane Ettifouri,Ayoub Belfatmi,Mahaman Sanoussi Yahaya Alassan,Walid Dahhane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MGT-B机制，通过监控自回归模型推理过程中的不确定性并触发回溯重解码，以提升小模型在长轨迹中的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化小型自回归推理模型易陷入重复或无效轨迹，而现有推理计算分配未观察轨迹发展情况。

Method: 开发MGT-B外部控制器，基于预采样不确定性和退化特征映射到条件尾部概率，利用CUSUM重置累积混合投注因子；当触发警报时，估算回滚点，恢复token和KV缓存状态并进行受限重解码。采用时间顺序审计集（240对）和历史覆盖集（467对）进行评估。

Result: 在240对严格审计集中，准确率从82/240提升至88/240（+2.50%），McNemar p=0.2632（不显著）。在467对探索性集合中，准确率从146/467提升至167/467（+4.50%，p=0.00075显著），但包含阈值选择前的数据。无警报输出与基线一致，有警报轨迹中有29次修正和8次退化。

Conclusion: 结果支持在特定MATH-500设置下使用选择性监控与修复机制，而非通用的理论认证推理改进；实证因素尚未被确立为有效的e过程或e检测器。

Abstract: Quantized small autoregressive reasoning models can enter long, repetitive, or unproductive trajectories, yet inference-time compute is usually allocated without observing how a trajectory develops. Building on an earlier token-level e-CUSUM controller, we develop MGT-B (Monitoring-Guided Test-time Backtracking), a revised external controller that maps overlapping windows of pre-sampling uncertainty and degeneration features to position-conditional empirical tail probabilities, accumulates mixture betting factors with a CUSUM-shaped reset, and responds to an alarm by estimating a rollback point, restoring token and key-value-cache state, and performing constrained re-decoding. To audit whether the effect persists on problem identities first observed after the manual choice of log threshold h = 10, we retrospectively exclude 260 IDs present in pre-threshold artifacts and retain the chronologically first post-threshold pair for each remaining ID, yielding a 240-pair chronology-audit set. On this set, accuracy changes from 82/240 to 88/240 (+2.50 percentage points; 13 corrections, 7 regressions; exact McNemar p = 0.2632; paired bootstrap 95% interval [-1.25, +6.25]). A broader 467-pair historical-coverage set of seed-matched pairs changes accuracy from 146/467 to 167/467 (+4.50 points; McNemar p = 0.000753), but includes 200 seed-1 IDs available before or during threshold selection and is reported only as an exploratory estimate. All 316 no-alarm outputs in the 467-pair set are identical to vanilla, while the 151 alarmed trajectories contain 29 corrections and 8 regressions. Neither analysis is confirmatory, and the empirical factors are not established as a valid e-process or e-detector. The results support a selective monitoring-and-repair mechanism for the studied MATH-500 setting, rather than a general or theoretically certified reasoning improvement.

</details>


### [167] [PoTRE: Test-Time Reasoning inspired by Cognitive Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20268)
*Anmol Kankariya,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PoTRE框架，通过解耦推理为四个异构智能体（对抗优化、分层规划、谱搜索、直接链式），并由任务自适应聚合层动态整合结果，在ARC-AGI-2、HLE和PRBench Finance基准上取得SOTA性能，特别是在HLE上达到49.92%准确率，且推理效率优于大规模同质基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型（LLMs）在处理需要长期规划和迭代纠错的复杂推理任务时的不足，以及标准单流提示在面对新颖抽象或严格领域约束时的脆弱性问题。

Method: 引入PoTRE（Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles）异构框架，将推理过程解耦为四个专用智能体：(1) 对抗优化智能体，(2) 分层战略规划智能体，(3) 谱搜索智能体，(4) 直接链式智能体。随后通过最终的任务自适应聚合层，利用候选选择、语义合成或神经符号验证等方式动态协调这些视角，生成稳健的全局解决方案。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-2、Humanity's Last Exam (HLE) 和 PRBench Finance三个前沿基准上进行评估。PoTRE在HLE上取得了49.92%的最高准确率，超越了此前的官方最佳成绩。研究表明，该架构异构性在相似或更少的推理token消耗下，实现了比重度扩展的同质基线更好的推理性能。

Conclusion: 通过构建异构的多智能体推理框架并采用动态聚合机制，可以有效提升LLM在复杂推理任务中的表现，同时保持较高的计算效率。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel at many tasks, they frequently struggle with complex reasoning that requires long-horizon planning and iterative error correction. Furthermore, standard single-stream prompting proves brittle when models encounter novel abstractions or rigorous domain constraints. We introduce PoTRE (Poly-Topological Reasoning Ensembles), a heterogeneous framework that decouples inference into four agents: (1) Adversarial Refinement Agent, (2) Hierarchical strategic Planning Agent, (3) Spectrum Search Agent, and (4) Direct Chain Agent. A final Task-Adaptive Aggregation Layer dynamically reconciles these perspectives -- via final candidate selection, semantic synthesis, or neuro-symbolic verification -- to produce a robust global solution. We evaluate PoTRE on three frontier benchmarks: ARC-AGI-2, Humanity's Last Exam (HLE), and PRBench Finance. PoTRE achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 49.92% on HLE, surpassing the previous best official score. We demonstrate that this architectural heterogeneity achieves improved reasoning performance using similar or fewer inference tokens compared to heavily scaled homogeneous baselines.

</details>


### [168] [Train the Model, Not the Reader: Decodability Supervision for Verifiable Activation Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20379)
*Hiskias Dingeto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出自然语言自动编码器通过重建来评估解释的忠实度存在结构性缺陷，容易受到虚假声明的影响。为此提出RECAP方法，通过辅助预测器保持内部内容的可解码性，从而提供更可靠的解释验证机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于重建的自然语言自动编码器在评估隐藏激活的解释时，对个体虚假声明不敏感，导致无法准确区分真实事实与虚假陈述，存在被模型操纵的风险。

Method: 提出两种审计协议（grounded-vs-true cross和evaluator swap）以及RECAP（Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors），即在与目标模型共同训练的同时训练线性头部，以保持指定内容的可解码性。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5-7B和Pythia-160M上验证了现有方法的局限性；RECAP使指定内容可通过探针可靠解码，且能显著区分真实声明与虚假声明（AUC从0.82提升至0.96），即使面对最大化重建分数的恶意编辑也能有效识别谎言。

Conclusion: 高重建得分并不能保证单个声明的真实性；RECAP提供了一种独立于文本表述的内部内容检查机制，提高了AI安全领域中解释验证的可靠性。

Abstract: Natural-language autoencoders score explanations of hidden activations by reconstruction: an explanation is deemed faithful if the activation can be regenerated from it. The test is structurally insensitive to individual false claims: if flipping a claim does not change the reconstruction, the claim is never penalized. We show the test is passed in two ways, neither faithful. On a released Qwen-2.5-7B verbalizer, explanations reconstruct well above chance while ~2% of specific claims are reconstruction-dependent, so the score tracks gist, not specific facts. Under exact synthetic ground truth, the standard recipe develops co-adapted private codes (false wording the reconstruction depends on) in 5/5 runs, and fixes that leave the target model unchanged do not help. We contribute two audit protocols, the grounded-vs-true cross and the evaluator swap, and RECAP (Readable Encodings via Co-trained Auxiliary Predictors): linear heads trained alongside the target model to keep designated content decodable. On RECAP-trained sandbox models, fresh verbalizers state the designated content truly and the codes vanish, at a +0.001-nat cost. This replicates on a pretrained Pythia-160M: the content becomes reliably probe-decodable, though a fresh verbalizer conveys it only in part (truth 0.44-0.46 vs a near-zero control). For interpretability, high reconstruction does not certify individual claims. For AI safety, RECAP makes designated internal content independently checkable against probes rather than asserted by prose a model can game: an independent probe scores the verbalizer's true claims above its false ones (AUC 0.96, vs 0.82 without RECAP). Against an adversary that edits an explanation to maximize the reconstruction score while lying (suppressing ~87% of its lie penalty), the RECAP probe still flags the lies (AUC 0.95) while the control probe collapses to chance (0.51).

</details>


### [169] [SoftReason: A Fully Differentiable Neuro-Soft-Symbolic Deductive Reasoning Architecture over High-Dimensional Perceptual Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20402)
*Wael AbdAlmageed*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SoftReason，一种神经软符号架构，用于在潜在感知事实和知识图谱提供的谓词上进行可微演绎推理，消除传统神经符号管道中的梯度断裂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典神经符号管道中感知与演绎之间离散接口导致的梯度断裂问题，实现从高分辨率输入推断前提并与知识图谱结合的可微推理。

Method: 将演绎状态表示为候选常量和谓词的局部软解释张量；利用谓词定义嵌入和潜在组合通道形成软体-谓词混合体，聚合所有可能的见证者，通过单调概率OR更新解释，实现可微的即时后果算子提升。

Result: 构建了端到端可训练的架构，支持感知接地、知识图谱证据注入和可微演绎闭合，并在知识感知视觉问答（KVQA）任务中得到实例化验证。

Conclusion: SoftReason成功实现了从感知到逻辑推理的全链路可微化，为神经符号推理提供了一种新的统一框架。

Abstract: In many reasoning problems, the premises are not observed as discrete symbols, but must be inferred from high-dimensional inputs. Further, the predicate vocabulary, argument structure, and trusted evidence are supplied by a Knowledge Graph (KG), or rule definitions. Classical neuro-symbolic pipelines have a discrete interface between perception and deduction. We present a neuro-soft-symbolic architecture for differentiable deductive reasoning over latent perceptual facts and knowledge-provided predicates. SoftReason removes the gradient gap by representing the deductive state as a local soft interpretation tensor over candidate constants and predicates. Perception proposes probabilistic base facts, KG triples enter as high-confidence soft evidence, and every query anchor, predicate choice, and closure update remains differentiable. Our core innovation is a learned differentiable lift of the immediate-consequence operator. It uses predicate-definition embeddings and latent composition channels to form soft body-predicate mixtures, aggregate over all possible witnesses, propose query-conditioned head facts, and update the interpretation through a monotone probabilistic OR. We instantiate the framework on Knowledge-aware Visual Question Answering (KVQA), and demonstrates how SoftReason supports end-to-end perceptual grounding, KG evidence injection, and differentiable deductive closure in one trainable architecture.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Hollywood: Towards a Large Movie Dataset for Database Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19666)
*Ivan Iachnyk,Mihail Stoian,Andreas Kipf*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出Hollywood，一个合成IMDb兼容基准生成器，结合LLM语义字典和确定性时序图关系数据生成，以解决原始IMDb数据集缺乏比例因子的问题。实验显示其在基数估计误差上与真实IMDb相当或更优，支持评估估计器的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的IMDb真实世界数据集广泛用于基数估计研究，但作为简单导出文件，缺乏类似TPC系列的规模因子（scale factor），限制了其在不同数据规模下的测试能力。

Method: 开发Hollywood基准生成器，利用大语言模型（LLM）生成的语义字典与基于确定性时序图的关系统计数据生成技术相结合，创建合成数据。构建了包含20万电影、1970万行数据和213个查询的Hollywood-200K初步版本。

Result: 在两个开放系统上的实验表明，Hollywood产生的基数估计误差与原始IMDb数据集相当甚至更高。发布了生成设置、提示/LLM输出溯源、适配SQL及标签，用于测试基数估计器是否能在固定电影快照和分布之外进行泛化。

Conclusion: Hollywood提供了一个可扩展、可复现的合成基准，弥补了传统IMDb数据集在规模灵活性上的不足，为评估基数估计器的鲁棒性和泛化能力提供了新工具。

Abstract: The IMDb real-world dataset of the JOB benchmark has been extensively used in the last decade as part of the research line on cardinality estimation, given its ability to stress test both traditional and learned estimators. However, unlike the synthetic TPC family, it does not come with a scale factor, being a simple dump. We introduce Hollywood, a synthetic IMDb-compatible benchmark generator that combines LLM-generated semantic dictionaries with deterministic temporal-graph-based relational data generation. We analyze a preliminary Hollywood-200K, which contains 200,000 primary movies, generated series and episode title rows, 19.7M IMDb-style rows, and 213 nonzero JOB-Light, JOB, and JOB-Complex queries. Experiments with two open systems demonstrate that Hollywood induces cardinality estimation errors comparable to or exceeding those observed on the original IMDb dataset. The release includes generation settings and prompt/LLM-output provenance together with adapted SQL and labels, enabling tests of whether cardinality estimators generalize beyond a fixed movie snapshot and distribution.

</details>


### [171] [Benchmarking the Full Pipeline of Materialized-View-Based Query Rewriting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19679)
*Xinjie Hu,Zhengjie Miao*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文通过模块化评估框架和控制性消融实验，对物化视图（MV）查询重写的全流程（候选枚举、视图选择、查询重写）进行了基准测试。研究揭示了各阶段间的强交互效应及系统间巨大差异，识别了导致性能退化的常见故障模式，为未来MV设计提供了指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MV查询加速评估通常仅关注流水线中的部分环节或在单一系统内进行，缺乏对端到端权衡和跨系统行为的清晰理解。

Method: 构建模块化评估框架，联合评估枚举、选择和重写三个阶段；引入跨引擎协议，对比原生优化器级重写与可移植SQL重写基线；在代表性学术方法及现代开源/商业系统上进行基准测试。

Result: 发现各阶段间存在强烈的交互效应，不同系统在MV使用率和实际节省效果上差异巨大；识别了解释重写后性能退化的重复性故障模式；明确了限制性能的主要流水线阶段。

Conclusion: 研究结果为未来的MV枚举、选择和重写设计提供了实证依据，强调了全面评估全流程的重要性。

Abstract: Materialized views (MVs) accelerate OLAP and data-warehouse workloads by precomputing reusable subexpressions, but practical MV-based query acceleration is a multi-stage pipeline: candidate enumeration, view selection under storage budgets, and query rewriting inside the optimizer. Existing evaluations typically study only parts of this pipeline and within a single system, leaving end-to-end trade-offs and cross-system behavior unclear.
  In this paper, we benchmark MV-based query rewriting by jointly evaluating enumeration, selection, and rewriting with a modular evaluation framework and by using controlled ablations. We also introduce a cross-engine protocol allowing us to compare systems that expose only execution plans by contrasting native optimizer-level rewriting with portable SQL rewriting baselines when available. Across representative academic methods and modern open-source and commercial systems, we find strong interaction effects across stages and large variability in MV usage and realized savings. We identify recurring failure modes that explain performance regressions after rewriting. Our results highlight which pipeline stages most often limit performance and provide evidence to guide future MV enumeration, selection, and rewriting designs.

</details>


### [172] [RCC: Speculative Write Versioning with Redo Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19697)
*Hyejin Yoo,Seongjae Moon,Sang-Won Lee,Jonghyeok Park*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出RCC（Redo-log-based Conflict Resolution），利用重做日志解决写-写冲突，通过原地外更新和延迟安装策略实现高并发，显著提升TPC-C和YCSB基准测试性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代OLTP引擎虽能通过多版本控制消除读-写冲突，但在处理写-写冲突时并发能力受限。传统方法因记录原地更新导致事务串行化，限制了系统吞吐量。

Method: 1. **推测性写入**：事务通过重做日志创建记录的推测性版本，而非直接修改原数据。2. **延迟更新策略**：仅在提交时将更新安装到记录中，回滚时直接丢弃推测版本，实现轻量级回滚。3. **两项关键技术**：(a) 提交时死锁检测，仅在提交时惰性检测循环；(b) RCC-C，利用列粒度重做日志消除记录级的假写-写冲突。4. **一致性保证**：基于锁的访问时冲突排序和依赖图跟踪确保可串行化。5. **集成实现**：在MySQL和PostgreSQL中集成该机制至缓冲管理器、锁管理器和恢复模块。

Result: 在128核机器上运行64个并发线程时，RCC相比Vanilla版本将TPC-C的事务吞吐量和延迟改善了一个数量级。RCC-C通过避免假冲突导致的死锁和不必要中止进一步提升了性能。在高争用YCSB基准测试中，RCC能扩展至128客户端，而竞争方案无法超过32线程。

Conclusion: RCC通过结合重做日志与推测性版本控制，有效解决了OLTP系统中的写-写冲突瓶颈，实现了高并发下的可扩展性和高性能，证明了在现有关系型数据库引擎中集成此类机制的可行性与优势。

Abstract: Modern OLTP engines rely on multi-versioning to eliminate read-write conflicts, yet their concurrency is severely limited for write-write conflicts. The conventional wisdom of updating records in place and immediately causes only one transaction to update a record at a time, and other update-conflicting transactions to wait for the former to commit or abort. Thus, conflicting transactions are serialized. We propose RCC, which leverages redo logs to resolve conflicting writes. A transaction updates a record out of place by creating a speculative write version using the redo log. With multiple speculative versions, RCC allows concurrent transactions to be pipelined after the update-conflicting point. Each update made by a transaction is installed to its record upon commit. This lazy update policy enables lightweight rollback: a transaction aborts by discarding its speculative versions. To realize the performance potential of its speculative versioning, RCC proposes two novel techniques: commit-time deadlock detection and columnar RCC (RCC-C). The former detects cycles only once lazily at commit time and the latter eliminates record-level false WW conflicts by leveraging column-granule redo logs. RCC guarantees serializability using lock-based access-time conflict ordering and dependency graph tracking. We implement RCC on MySQL and PostgreSQL, integrating the concurrency control mechanism with their buffer managers, lock managers, and recovery modules. RCC improves TPC-C's transaction throughput and latency over Vanilla versions by an order of magnitude when running 64 concurrent threads on a machine with 128 cores. RCC-C further boosts throughput and latency by avoiding false conflict-induced deadlocks and unnecessary aborts. For a high-contention YCSB benchmark, commit-time deadlock detection enables RCC to scale to 128 clients while competing schemes do not scale beyond 32 threads.

</details>


### [173] [Extending GouDa: Generation of Universal Datasets with (and without) Errors for Data Quality Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20165)
*Valerie Restat,André Conrad,Kevin M. Kramer,Uta Störl*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文介绍了名为 GouDa 的数据生成器，旨在解决合成数据生成中真实性、边缘案例覆盖、可控错误插入及多格式支持等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成数据在数据质量、清洗和机器学习中至关重要，但现有方法难以同时满足高真实度、覆盖边缘案例、支持可控错误插入以及兼容多种数据格式（如表格数据和 NoSQL）的需求。

Method: 提出 GouDa 数据生成器，通过广泛的生成函数和用户自定义属性值列表，实现不同数据格式的兼容、受控错误注入及基准真值（ground truth）的生成。

Result: GouDa 能够生成符合多种用例要求的逼真数据，支持不同数据格式，允许精确控制错误插入，并提供无错误的基准数据版本。

Conclusion: GouDa 有效满足了合成数据生成的多重复杂需求，为数据分析和机器学习提供了灵活且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: Synthetic data is extremely important in areas such as data quality, data cleaning, and machine learning. It enables the analysis of use cases in which real data is insufficient, unavailable, or distorted. However, generating synthetic data also presents challenges: The data must be as realistic as possible, but at the same time cover edge cases. It must be possible to insert controlled errors, and at the same time, an error-free version of the data is usually required. Additionally, it is necessary to consider numerous data formats, such as tabular data, but also NoSQL data models. To this end, we present our data generator GouDa. GouDa precisely meets these requirements - it is suitable for different data formats, enables the controlled insertion of errors, and generates ground truth. A wide range of different generation functions and the option to add your own lists of possible attribute values allow the generation of realistic data that covers many different use cases.

</details>


### [174] [Worst-Case Optimal BGPs on Temporal Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20356)
*Diego Arroyuelo,Aidan Hogan,Gonzalo Navarro,Juan Reutter*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对带时间区间标签图的基本图模式（BGP）的最坏情况最优（WCO）评估方法，通过构建O(N)空间的索引结构，结合改进的Leapfrog Triejoin算法，实现了在WCO时间内高效处理含时间约束的复杂查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理具有时间有效性区间的动态/时序图上的基本图模式查询时，缺乏在最坏情况下的最优性能保证，且难以灵活支持包含时间常数或变量顺序关系的复杂查询语言。

Method: 1. 设计了一种新的索引结构，存储N条边的时序图仅需O(N)空间；2. 将Leapfrog Triejoin算法适配到时序图场景，支持任意变量求值顺序；3. 引入扩展BGP查询语言，允许用户指定形如(subject, property, object, time)的四元组及时间关系约束；4. 利用该索引实现最坏情况最优（WCO）的时间复杂度评估，即O(Q* m log N)，其中Q*为最大解数量。

Result: 该方法不仅对扩展BGP提供WCO保证，还适用于快照评估、版本查询等其他时序变体。实验表明，在真实世界数据集上，该方法能以毫秒级响应时间回答现实查询，且空间开销较低。

Conclusion: 所提出的索引结构和算法框架能够高效、最优地评估时序图中的复杂图模式查询，在保持低空间占用的同时显著提升查询响应速度，具有良好的实用性和理论保障。

Abstract: We study how to evaluate basic graph patterns (BGPs) in a worst-case-optimal (wco) manner over {\em temporal} labeled graphs, where edges have an interval of temporal validity. We adopt a flexible query language in which users specify m quads of the form (subject, property, object, time), using constants or variables. The time component denotes the instant at which a particular edge is valid, and users may also include order relations between temporal constants or variables. The answer is the set of all valid variable assignments, including time. We describe an index structure that, for a temporal graph with N edges, requires O(N) space and can evaluate extended BGPs in wco time O(Q* m log N), where Q* represents the maximum number of solutions for query Q over any temporal graph with the same number of instants of edge validity. We use our index to adapt Leapfrog Triejoin to the temporal graph setting under any variable evaluation ordering. Our index further yields wco guarantees for related query types, including snapshot evaluation, version queries, and other temporal variants. Experiments on real-world datasets show that our approach answers realistic queries in milliseconds with low space overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [Personalized Recommendation Tool Learning via Autonomous Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19739)
*Mingdai Yang,Zhiwei Liu,Weizhi Zhang,Yibo Wang,Hao Peng,Philip Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出PRTA框架，利用LLM作为中央规划器选择传统推荐模型作为工具，解决LLM在推荐系统中的幻觉和上下文限制问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐智能体存在幻觉和上下文长度限制，不适合全排序推荐任务；旨在通过架构设计而非修改LLM本身来克服这些局限。

Method: 构建基于代理的推荐框架PRTA，LLM负责高层推理和个性化工具选择，传统推荐模型执行全排序打分；设计反射机制让智能体根据用户画像和候选列表评估比较不同工具。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在全排序推荐性能上优于传统的推荐方法和基于LLM的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM的传统推理能力与专用推荐模型的扩展性相结合，PRTA有效提升了全排序推荐的准确性和个性化水平。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) have recently gained traction in recommender systems due to their strong reasoning capabilities and extensive world knowledge, previous LLM-based agents suffer from hallucination and context-length limitations, and thus are not suitable for full-ranking recommendation tasks. To circumvent these limitations through architectural design rather than modifying the LLM itself, we propose an agent-based recommendation framework, memory-based $\textbf{P}$ersonalized $\textbf{R}$ecommendation $\textbf{T}$ool learning via autonomous language $\textbf{A}$gents (PRTA), in which an LLM acts as a central planner interacting with multiple recommendation models as tools. The LLM-based agent is responsible for high-level reasoning and personalized tool selection, while traditional recommendation models perform full-ranking scoring, leveraging their scalability in modeling behavioral patterns. To support personalized tool selection, we design reflection mechanisms that enable the agent to evaluate and compare tools for each user based on user profiles and candidate ranked lists. Extensive experiments across three public datasets demonstrate the superiority of \modelname over traditional recommendation and LLM-based baselines in improving full-ranking recommendation performance.

</details>


### [176] [CIR at iKAT SCAI 2026: Exploring Clarification Need Prediction in Agentic Conversational Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19801)
*Nolwenn Bernard,Jüri Keller,Philipp Schaer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 科隆信息检索小组在iKAT SCAI 2026共享任务中展示了基于智能体的对话式搜索系统，该系统具备查询重写、检索、重排序、答案生成及澄清需求预测等功能，并实验了两种不同的神经澄清需求预测模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参与iKAT SCAI 2026共享任务，展示并评估智能体对话式搜索系统在复杂搜索场景下的性能，特别是其在处理需要澄清的查询时的能力。

Method: 构建一个配备多种工具的智能体对话式搜索系统，包括查询重写、检索、重排序、答案生成、澄清需求预测和澄清问题生成。重点实验了两种不同的神经澄清需求预测模型。

Result: 成功参与了iKAT SCAI 2026共享任务，并对比了两种神经澄清需求预测模型在该系统中的表现。

Conclusion: 智能体对话式搜索系统结合先进的澄清需求预测模型，能够有效提升处理模糊或歧义查询的能力，为未来对话式搜索引擎的设计提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: This paper presents the participation of the Cologne Information Retrieval group in the iKAT SCAI 2026 shared task. We use an agentic conversational search system, equipped with tools for query rewriting, retrieval and reranking, answer generation, and clarification need prediction and clarification question generation. We experiment with two different neural clarification need prediction models.

</details>


### [177] [Zero-Observation User Reactivation with Gap-Driven Dimensional Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19802)
*Jiandong Ding,Tianying Liu,Fuyuan Liu,Huijie Qin,Tiandeng Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DeltaGate，一种轻量级输出层插件，通过根据时间间隔Delta t和个性化历史动态路由表示维度，解决长期未交互用户的推荐性能下降问题。在Amazon数据集上显著优于基线模型，且保持骨干网络冻结，参数开销极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序推荐（SR）模型难以处理用户长时间无交互的情况（Zero-Observation Reactivation）。随着观察间隔Delta t增加，推荐准确率单调下降，尤其在超过一年后达到最低点。现有方法在重新训练时会改变骨干嵌入，导致不稳定和高计算成本。

Method: 提出DeltaGate：1) 轻量级输出层插件，不修改预训练骨干网络；2) 将每个表示维度在“个性化历史”与“零初始化的全局先验”之间进行路由；3) Gate机制同时条件化于时间间隔Delta t和用户个性化表示；4) 采用Gap-Synthesize Protocol在三个Amazon数据集上进行评估。

Result: 1) 在Video Games数据集中，>365天间隔下，DG-SASRec Hit@10为0.047 vs SASRec的0.031；DG-BERT4Rec为0.046 vs BERT4Rec的0.025；2) 仅增加66K可训练参数（2-4%开销）；3) 相比端到端重训练，冻结插件保留零骨干漂移，参数量少约40倍，并实现可观测的维度级路由。

Conclusion: DeltaGate有效缓解了长期无交互导致的推荐性能衰退，以极低参数代价显著提升长尾时间间隔下的推荐准确性，且保持模型稳定性和可解释性。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation (SR) models capture continuously observed behavior, but a returning user may have no interactions for months or years. We define this setting as Zero-Observation Reactivation: the user has a pre-gap history, while the platform observes no behavioral signals during a macro-gap Delta t. Under a chronologically aligned Gap-Synthesize Protocol on three Amazon datasets (Video Games, CDs & Vinyl, and Movies & TV), Hit@10 decreases monotonically across the evaluated gap buckets and reaches its lowest level beyond one year. The pattern appears across recurrent, unidirectional, and bidirectional SR backbones.
  We propose DeltaGate, a lightweight output-layer plugin that keeps the backbone frozen and routes each representation dimension between the personalized history and a learned, zero-initialized global prior. The gate is conditioned jointly on Delta t and the personalized representation. In a controlled diagnostic, we hold the personalized representation fixed and vary Delta t to isolate the trained gate's response to the gap input. In the >365d Video Games bucket, DG-SASRec reaches 0.047 Hit@10 versus 0.031 for SASRec, while DG-BERT4Rec reaches 0.046 versus 0.025 for BERT4Rec, with 66K trainable parameters (2--4% overhead). End-to-end retraining attains higher absolute accuracy but changes the backbone embeddings; the frozen plugin preserves zero backbone drift, uses about 40x fewer trainable parameters, and retains observable dimension-wise routing. The source code is available at https://github.com/jdding/DeltaGate.

</details>


### [178] [Using Hierarchical Controlled Vocabularies to Understand CLIP Retrieval Failures in Historical Photo Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19836)
*Ratan Sebastian,Anett Hoppe,Christoph Rippe,Ralph Ewerth*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了AAT词汇表的结构属性（根面类型和层级深度）如何解释CLIP模型在GLAM机构图像检索中的表现差异，发现视觉一致性与文本-图像对齐性共同决定失败模式，且微调对浅层术语提升更显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已知CLIP性能受概念抽象度和频率影响，但缺乏从GLAM机构已使用的受控词汇表（如AAT）结构属性角度解释其性能差异的工作。

Method: 分析了三个使用AAT标注的历史照片集合，评估了视觉一致性、文本-图像对齐性及标准检索指标，并考察了根面类型和层级深度这两个结构属性的影响，同时评估了微调的效果。

Result: 1. 视觉一致性与文本-图像对齐性几乎不相关，联合区分了不同的失败模式；标签远离簇的术语检索效果最差。2. 根面类型显著分离了具有不同视觉一致性的类别，而检索指标与结构属性无显著相关。3. 微调整体改善检索，但对层级较浅的术语提升更大，主要源于文本-图像对齐性的改善。

Conclusion: AAT的结构属性（特别是根面类型和层级深度）有助于理解CLIP检索行为的差异，微调策略应针对不同层级术语进行优化。

Abstract: GLAM institutions (Galleries, Libraries, Archives, and Museums) organise image access using controlled vocabularies such as the Art and Architecture Thesaurus (AAT). For content-based image retrieval in these settings, vision-language models like CLIP are increasingly used, but their performance varies. This variation is known to relate to measures like concept abstraction and concept frequency. However, no prior work explains this variation in terms of the structural properties of vocabularies like the AAT that GLAM professionals already use. The AAT groups concepts into broad facets (Objects, Activities, Agents, etc.) and arranges terms hierarchically within them. In this paper, we ask whether two structural properties (root facet type and hierarchy depth) explain where CLIP retrieval succeeds and fails, and where fine-tuning helps. Across three historical photographic collections annotated with AAT terms, we examine visual coherence (whether a term's photographs cluster in CLIP's embedding space), text-image alignment (whether its label is near that cluster), and standard retrieval measures, which conflate the two. We find that visual coherence and text-image alignment are nearly uncorrelated across terms and jointly separate distinct failure modes. Terms whose photographs cluster tightly but whose label is distant from the cluster retrieve poorly in every collection, in two of three collections even worse than terms that fail on both metrics. We also show that while retrieval metrics do not correlate significantly with either structural property, root facet type does significantly separate categories with varying visual coherence. Finally, we find that fine-tuning improves retrieval overall, but its gains favour shallower terms in the hierarchy, where text-image alignment improves most, beyond what concept frequency explains.

</details>


### [179] [UniRank: Benchmarking Ranking Models for Unified Sequential Modeling and Feature Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19987)
*Honghao Li,Xianquan Wang,Zibin Zhang,Yi Zhang,Kangyi Lin,Yiwen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: UniRank是一个开放的排名模型基准，旨在统一序列建模与特征交互，提供可复现的实验环境、标准化的评估流程及高效的PyTorch工具包，用于在大规模公开数据集上比较15种代表性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有排名模型进步多依赖专有数据、封闭实现和大规模工业基础设施，导致难以进行可复现的比较，阻碍了学术界对缩放定律（scaling laws）、长序列建模和多任务排名的研究。

Method: 提出UniRank基准，采用时序逐点自回归监督；标准化反馈任务的评估；提供包含分布式数据并行、算子优化、混合精度训练等效率技术的PyTorch工具包以降低硬件门槛；在五个大型公开数据集（最大7亿实例，最长序列超10^5）上对15种统一排名模型进行基准测试。

Result: 成功建立了统一排名模型的可复现比较基础，能够在有限计算资源下研究缩放定律，并缩小了学术研究与工业界排名研究之间的差距。

Conclusion: UniRank通过提供可复现的实验、面向生产的评估标准和易用的实现方案，为研究人员、实践者和初学者带来了显著益处，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Ranking is a core stage in online advertising and recommender systems. Modern ranking models increasingly unify sequential modeling and feature interaction, yet many advances rely on proprietary data, closed implementations, and large-scale industrial infrastructure. This setting limits reproducible comparison and hinders academic study of scaling laws, long-sequence modeling, and multi-task ranking. To address these limitations, this paper proposes UniRank, an open benchmark for ranking models that unify sequential modeling and feature interaction. UniRank uses chronological pointwise autoregressive supervision, standardizes evaluation across feedback tasks, and provides a PyTorch toolkit with Distributed Data Parallel training, operator optimization, mixed-precision training, attention optimization, and other efficiency techniques that reduce hardware requirements. We benchmark 15 representative unified ranking models on five large-scale public datasets from short-video, advertising, and e-commerce platforms, with the largest dataset containing over 700 million instances and the longest behavior sequence exceeding 10^5 interactions. UniRank provides a reproducible basis for comparing unified ranking models, studying scaling laws under limited compute, and narrowing the gap between academic and industrial ranking research. We believe UniRank benefits researchers, practitioners, and beginners through reproducible experiments, production-oriented evaluation, and accessible implementations. Code and data are available at https://github.com/salmon1802/UniRank.

</details>
